        n = len(A)
        dp = [0] + [float('inf')] * n
        for i in range(n):
            C = [0] * n
            val = K

                C[A[j]] += 1
                dp[i + 1] = min(dp[i + 1], dp[j] + val)

class Solution:
    def minCost(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> int:
        n = len(nums)
        dp = [0] + [inf]*n
        for i in range(n):
            cnt = defaultdict(int)
            dup = 0
            for j in range(i, -1, -1):
                cnt[nums[j]] += 1
                if cnt[nums[j]] == 2:
                    dup += 2
                elif cnt[nums[j]] > 2:
                    dup += 1
                dp[i+1] = min(dp[i+1], dp[j] + k + dup)
        return dp[-1]
```

class Solution:
    def minCost(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> int:
        n = len(nums)
        dp = [0] + [inf]*n
        for i in range(n):
            cnt = {}
            dup = 0
            for j in range(i, -1, -1):
                cnt[nums[j]] = cnt.get(nums[j], 0) + 1
                if cnt[nums[j]] == 2:
                    dup += 2
                elif cnt[nums[j]] > 2:
                    dup += 1
                dp[i+1] = min(dp[i+1], dp[j] + k + dup)
        return dp[-1]
```

```
int minCost(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
    int n = nums.size();
    vector<int> dp(n+1, 1e9);
    dp[0] = 0;
    for (int i=0; i<n; ++i) {
        vector<int> cnt(n+1);
        int cost = k;
        for (int j=i; j>=0; --j) {
            if (++cnt[nums[j]] == 2) cost += 2;
            else if (cnt[nums[j]] > 2) cost += 1;
            dp[i+1] = min(dp[i+1], dp[j] + cost);
        }
    }
    return dp[n];
}
```

```
class Solution:
    def minCost(self, A: List[int], K: int) -> int:
        n = len(A)
        dp = [0] + [float('inf')] * n
        for i in range(n):
            cnt = {}
            val = K
            for j in range(i, -1, -1):
                x = A[j]
                if cnt.get(x, 0) == 1:
                    val += 2
                elif cnt.get(x, 0) >= 2:
                    val += 1
                cnt[x] = cnt.get(x, 0) + 1
                dp[i+1] = min(dp[i+1], dp[j] + val)
        return dp[-1]
```

class Solution:
    def minCost(self, A, K):
        n = len(A)
        dp = [0] + [float('inf')] * n
        for i in range(n):
            cnt = {}
            val = K
            for j in range(i, -1, -1):
                x = A[j]
                c = cnt.get(x, 0)
                if c == 1:
                    val += 2
                elif c >= 2:
                    val += 1
                cnt[x] = c + 1
                dp[i + 1] = min(dp[i + 1], dp[j] + val)
        return dp[-1]
```

Let's decide final raw code:
```
class Solution:
    def minCost(self, A, K):
        n = len(A)
        dp = [0] + [float('inf')] * n
        for i in range(n):
            cnt = {}
            val = K
            for j in range(i, -1, -1):
                x = A[j]
                c = cnt.get(x, 0)
                if c == 1:
                    val += 2
                elif c >= 2:
                    val += 1
                cnt[x] = c + 1
                dp[i + 1] = min(dp[i + 1], dp[j] + val)
        return dp[-1]
```

class Solution:
    def minCost(self, A, K):
        n = len(A)
        dp = [0] + [float('inf')] * n
        for i in range(n):
            cnt = {}
            val = K
            for j in range(i, -1, -1):

                c = cnt.get(x, 0)
                if c == 1:
                    val += 2
                elif c >= 2:
                    val += 1
                cnt[x] = c + 1
                dp[i + 1] = min(dp[i + 1], dp[j] + val)
        return dp[-1]
```

```
class Solution {
public:
    int minCost(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
        int n = nums.size();
        vector<int> dp(n + 1, 1e9);
        dp[0] = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            vector<int> cnt(n + 1);
            int cost = k;
            for (int j = i; j >= 0; j--) {
                if (++cnt[nums[j]] == 2) cost += 2;
                else if (cnt[nums[j]] > 2) cost += 1;
                dp[i+1] = min(dp[i+1], dp[j] + cost);
            }
        }
        return dp[n];
    }
};
```

```
int minCost(vector<int>& nums, int k) {
    int n = nums.size();
    vector<int> dp(n + 1, INT_MAX / 2);
    dp[0] = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        unordered_map<int, int> cnt;
        int cost = k;
        for (int j = i; j >= 0; --j) {
            cnt[nums[j]]++;
            if (cnt[nums[j]] == 2) cost += 2;
            else if (cnt[nums[j]] > 2) cost += 1;
            dp[i + 1] = min(dp[i + 1], dp[j] + cost);
        }
    }
    return dp[n];
}
```

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
class Solution:
    def minCost(self, A, K):
        n = len(A)
        dp = [0] + [float('inf')] * n
        for i in range(n):
            cnt = {}
            val = K
            for j in range(i, -1, -1):
                x = A[j]
                c = cnt.get(x, 0)
                if c == 1:
                    val += 2
                elif c >= 2:
                    val += 1
                cnt[x] = c + 1
                dp[i + 1] = min(dp[i + 1], dp[j] + val)
        return dp[-1]
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
class Solution:
    def minCost(self, A, K):
        n = len(A)
        dp = [0] + [float('inf')] * n
        for i in range(n):
            cnt = {}
            val = K
            for j in range(i, -1, -1):
                x = A[j]
                c = cnt.get(x, 0)
                if c == 1:
                    val += 2
                elif c >= 2:
                    val += 1
                cnt[x] = c + 1
                dp[i + 1] = min(dp[i + 1], dp[j] + val)
        return dp[-1]
